No external libraries, answer caching, input-specific outputs, or evaluator changes. All packing and allocation stays inside gemm. Advice is unverified, not a measured fact. Do not infer causality from noisy scores or attribute joint outcomes to one advisor. 
Optimize standalone row-major FP32 C=A@B on Apple M4 Pro.
The user's current goal is to beat the imported baseline peak consistently over ten
runs. Recent imported peak was1685.6230608449698 GFLOP/s on AC Automatic; previous
Battery Automatic peak was1668.5964359101147. Measure ten validation reports and ten
fresh alternating paired controls for each candidate, not a lucky best sample.
Each report preserves the frozen hill's best-of-three timing rule. Historical scores
are context only. Whether the goal comparator is historical or fresh same-mode peak
awaits the user; report both, do not choose policy. Legacy internal1800 target remains
only an unresolved search setting; it does not define the new goal. Adoption still
requires the unchanged min(candidate)>1.05*max(paired controls) gate and held-out check.

Frozen ABI: void gemm(int n,const float*A,const float*B,float*C). Timing n512,
correct general sizes/tails/n<=0; clang21 -O3 -march=native -ffast-math -shared -fPIC -lm;
FP32 tolerance.002, single thread, no external library/runtime, no mixed precision,
input/result caching, scorer edits, precomputed transpose, or output-specific shortcut.
All input conversion, allocation/free, calls, SME transitions, and stores stay timed.
Preserve the complete LIBXSMM BSD notices. Inputs are fresh every call.

The baseline is unchanged full512 NN LIBXSMM gemm plus optional UNUSED helpers:
sera_libxsmm_panel32 and NEW full512 sera_libxsmm_ta, sera_libxsmm_tb,
sera_libxsmm_tt. Complete editable asm and declarations are in the supplied source.
They were imported offline from pinned LIBXSMM10490f10e79d4511f252c33279ef970a188cfab6,
not written by Sera. Their exact exported instruction bytes are verified in this
compiled source. Adding unused code changes binary layout; fresh baseline is mandatory.
The baseline still calls only sera_libxsmm_512. Augmented baseline passed48cases.
The three new primitives passed9 separate deterministic correctness tests with prepared
inputs, but no legal end-to-end wrapper has been measured. They are available options,
not selected experiments and not known speedups. The15specialists jointly rank proposals.

Each primitive accepts a zeroed const void*parameters[22], uses [4]=A_arg,[10]=B_arg,
[16]=C; full512cubed, FP32, beta0, lda/ldb/ldc512. To compute row-major C=A@B:
NN params4=B,params10=A; TA params4=transpose(B),params10=A;
TB params4=B,params10=transpose(A); TT params4=transpose(B),params10=transpose(A).
Here transpose(X) is a contiguous physical512x512FP32 transpose freshly computed inside
gemm; output C remains original. Each buffer is1MiB. New helpers own their SME
entry/exit. Existing sme_transpose can be inspected for possible reuse; no external
prototype or unavailable generator output can be invented. Preserve n!=512 fallback,
allocation-failure safety, original inputs, full overwrite and no persistent buffers.
TA retains generated B-stack packing and adds A-stack packing; TB skips B-stack
packing and does not add A-stack packing; TT skips B packing but adds A packing.
Do not infer performance from shorter code or one removed internal pack: required
external conversion costs are included, and traversal/layout/cache costs differ.

they review the shared board and jointly rank a batch. Astra implements that ranked
queue and reviews actual results. Root has not selected an experiment or descriptor.
Give exact blocks, pointer/layout mapping, conversion method, risks, and expected
measured effect. Distinguish complete algorithm/layout changes from redundant aliases.

pipeline variants without a genuinely different source change and hypothesis.

merely renaming a past rejected wrapper is not. If a ranked alias was already tested,
Astra should abstain rather than invent a new root-selected or unranked experiment.

Verified primitive mappings and generator behavior:
{"mapping-luna.md": "# Full-size LIBXSMM transpose-descriptor mappings\n\nThis records legal storage mappings for offline-generated full-size beta-zero primitives. It does not select or measure a candidate.\n\n## Fixed dimensions and parameter slots\n\nThe pinned exporter uses the LIBXSMM descriptor `(M,N,K)=(512,512,512)`, `BETA_0`, no prefetch, and the parameter block offsets `A=32`, `B=80`, `C=128` bytes (`params[4]`, `[10]`, and `[16]` on this 64-bit ABI). The offline exporter is [`export_kernel.c`](../cpu-libxsmm-reference-2026-09-24/export_kernel.c); its saved export metadata records those offsets. Transpose variants must add the matching `LIBXSMM_GEMM_FLAG_TRANS_A` and/or `LIBXSMM_GEMM_FLAG_TRANS_B` to `BETA_0` at dispatch.\n\nThe row-major contract is `C[i,j] = \u03a3k A[i,k] * B[k,j]`. LIBXSMM computes column-major `C_col = op(A_arg) * op(B_arg)`. View output memory as `C_col = C\u1d40`, so the required operations are `op(A_arg)=B\u1d40` and `op(B_arg)=A\u1d40`, with call dimensions `(M,N,K)=(n,m,k)`. Here all dimensions are 512, but the leading dimensions below are stated from the logical physical storage shapes.\n\n## Variants\n\nAll copied buffers contain FP32 values and are fully initialized before dispatch. Row-major input addresses below are `A_rm[i*k+j]` and `B_rm[i*n+j]`. The C pointer remains the original row-major `C`; interpreted column-major it is `C\u1d40`, with `ldc=n=512`. Because all dimensions are 512, every listed leading dimension is numerically 512.\n\n| Dispatch transpose flags | `params[4]`: physical A_arg storage and LD | `params[10]`: physical B_arg storage and LD | Required op(A_arg), op(B_arg) |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| none (current) | `B_rm`, stored as column-major `B\u1d40` of shape `n\u00d7k`; `lda=n` | `A_rm`, stored as column-major `A\u1d40` of shape `k\u00d7m`; `ldb=k` | `B\u1d40`, `A\u1d40` |\n| `TRANS_A` | `B_col_kn`, column-major logical `B` of shape `k\u00d7n`, with `B_col_kn[i + j*k] = B_rm[i*n+j]`; `lda=k` | `A_rm`, column-major logical `A\u1d40` of shape `k\u00d7m`; `ldb=k` | `B\u1d40`, `A\u1d40` |\n| `TRANS_B` | `B_rm`, column-major logical `B\u1d40` of shape `n\u00d7k`; `lda=n` | `A_col_mk`, column-major logical `A` of shape `m\u00d7k`, with `A_col_mk[i + j*m] = A_rm[i*k+j]`; `ldb=m` | `B\u1d40`, `A\u1d40` |\n| `TRANS_A & TRANS_B` | `B_col_kn` as above; `lda=k` | `A_col_mk` as above; `ldb=m` | `B\u1d40`, `A\u1d40` |\n\nThe output slot is `params[16]=C` in every row, with `ldc=n`. The beta-zero flag must remain set so each generated call overwrites its output. For this exact square case the transposed input buffers each occupy `512*512*sizeof(float)=1 MiB`.\n\n## Timing and correctness requirements\n\nThe current no-transpose primitive passes the original input pointers directly. Each enabled transpose flag requires its corresponding full buffer: `TRANS_A` requires a `B_col_kn` copy; `TRANS_B` requires an `A_col_mk` copy; both require both buffers. These are physical transposes, not free pointer aliases. Build and fill every required buffer inside `gemm` so allocation and all copy work remain inside the measured call. Preserve original A and B inputs, keep beta zero, write all of C, and free temporary buffers before return. If allocation failure is handled, use the unchanged no-transpose implementation as fallback rather than dispatching with a mismatched buffer.\n\nThe current exporter passes all leading dimensions as 512 because the workload is square. For a future non-square use, recompute physical dimensions and LDs from the table; do not copy the numeric 512 mechanically. Validate each exported descriptor's transpose flags and ABI slots against a small deterministic reference before using it in the fixed hill.\n\n## Source basis\n\n- Pinned LIBXSMM revision: `10490f10e79d4511f252c33279ef970a188cfab6`.\n- Existing exact no-transpose exporter: `evidence/cpu-libxsmm-reference-2026-09-24/export_kernel.c` and `export.json`.\n- Generator eligibility and transpose-to-stack paths: [`generator-review-luna.md`](../cpu-libxsmm-generator-2026-09-25/generator-review-luna.md), including links to pinned `generator_gemm.c` and `generator_gemm_sme.c`.\n- Algebraic identity: `C\u1d40=B\u1d40A\u1d40`.\n", "generator-paths-luna.md": "# LIBXSMM SME full-shape transpose descriptor paths\n\nSource inspected read-only: pinned LIBXSMM commit `10490f10e79d4511f252c33279ef970a188cfab6`, `src/generator_gemm.c` and `src/generator_gemm_sme.c`. The M4 dispatcher accepts FP32 XGEMM ABI descriptors with `BETA_0` plus none, either, or both of `TRANS_A` and `TRANS_B`; it strips beta-zero only for the supported-flag check and passes the descriptor to the SME blocking generator (`generator_gemm.c`, lines 1283\u20131295). These are legal generator descriptors, but they do not by themselves preserve the current row-major `C=A*B` mapping.\n\nFor the full 512\u00d7512\u00d7512 path, blocking gives 64-column N tiles and 32-row M tiles. In the main full-block branch, the generator handles B outside the M loop and A inside it (`generator_gemm_sme.c`, lines 651\u2013683). This makes the effects of the flags distinct:\n\n| Descriptor | Generator behavior relative to NN | Layout and implementation constraint |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `TRANS_A` only | **Adds A packing** through `transpose_a_to_stack` in each 32-row M block, while **retaining B packing** through `transpose_b_to_stack` for each 64-column N tile. The A transpose helper allocates a packed-A region and transposes K chunks (`generator_gemm_sme.c`, lines 285\u2013403); its call is at lines 679\u2013683. | No packing is removed. The flag changes `op(A)`, so the A pointer and storage must represent the matrix required by that transposed operation. Current row-major pointers cannot simply be reused. |\n| `TRANS_B` only | **Skips B packing to stack**. The N-tile setup takes the direct-B path instead of calling `transpose_b_to_stack` (`generator_gemm_sme.c`, lines 660\u2013671); later pointer updates also branch on the flag (lines 761\u2013770). It does not add A-transpose packing. | This is the only single-flag path that removes one NN packing operation. The B operand must be stored in the physical layout expected by `op(B)`; setting the flag on the present row-major `A` argument changes the mathematical operation and is incorrect without a matching remap/transform. |\n| `TRANS_A|TRANS_B` | **Skips B packing and adds A packing**. The transpose-A helper sizes its packed-A allocation with no B-stack component when `TRANS_B` is set (`generator_gemm_sme.c`, lines 343\u2013347), and the full-block M loop still calls that helper (lines 679\u2013683). | It exchanges which operand is packed; it is not a no-pack path. Both logical operations change, so both physical input layouts and pointer mapping need to be matched. Current pointers are not a valid drop-in. |\n\nIn NN, B is packed before each 64-column N tile and then reused by its M tiles; A is read in the generator\u2019s expected layout. Therefore source facts show that `TRANS_B` removes that generator B-stack packing path, while `TRANS_A` adds A-stack packing. The source does not establish whether any variant is faster after the required layout conversions. The timed call must include any input transformation, allocation, and cleanup. All three variants need an exported full-shape primitive, an explicit row-major mapping, guarded correctness against fresh inputs, and byte/relocation/license review before Sera can use them. No build, execution, benchmark, kernel edit, or speed inference was made for this note.\n"}

<stdlib.h>\n#include <stddef.h>\n\n/* Transpose through ZA: contiguous row loads become contiguous column stores.\n * The heap layout remains at[k*n+i], exactly as consumed by sme_gemm. */\n__attribute__((target(\"sme\")))\n__arm_new(\"za\") __arm_locally_streaming\nstatic void sme_transpose(int n, const float *a, float *at) {\n    const int vl = (int)svcntsw();\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; i += 2*vl) {\n        const int rows0 = n-i < vl ? n-i : vl;\n        const int rows1 = n-i-vl < vl ? n-i-vl : vl;\n        svbool_t pi0 = svwhilelt_b32(i, n);\n        svbool_t pi1 = svwhilelt_b32(i+vl, n);\n        for (int k = 0; k < n; k += 2*vl) {\n            const int cols0 = n-k < vl ? n-k : vl;\n            const int cols1 = n-k-vl < vl ? n-k-vl : vl;\n            svbool_t pk0 = svwhilelt_b32(k, n);\n            svbool_t pk1 = svwhilelt_b32(k+vl, n);\n            for (int r = 0; r < rows0; ++r) {\n                svld1_hor_za32(0, r, pk0, a+(size_t)(i+r)*n+k);\n                if (cols1 > 0)\n                    svld1_hor_za32(1, r, pk1, a+(size_t)(i+r)*n+k+vl);\n            }\n            for (int r = 0; r < rows1; ++r) {\n                svld1_hor_za32(2, r, pk0, a+(size_t)(i+vl+r)*n+k);\n                if (cols1 > 0)\n                    svld1_hor_za32(3, r, pk1, a+(size_t)(i+vl+r)*n+k+vl);\n            }\n            for (int s = 0; s < cols0; ++s) {\n                svst1_ver_za32(0, s, pi0, at+(size_t)(k+s)*n+i);\n                if (rows1 > 0)\n                    svst1_ver_za32(2, s, pi1, at+(size_t)(k+s)*n+i+vl);\n            }\n            for (int s = 0; s < cols1; ++s) {\n                svst1_ver_za32(1, s, pi0, at+(size_t)(k+vl+s)*n+i);\n                if (rows1 > 0)\n                    svst1_ver_za32(3, s, pi1, at+(size_t)(k+vl+s)*n+i+vl);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/* CPU SME outer products: four ZA tiles hold a 2VL x 2VL output block. */\n__attribute__((target(\"sme\")))\n__arm_new(\"za\") __arm_locally_streaming\nstatic void sme_gemm(int n,const float *at,const float *b,float *c) {\n    const int vl=(int)svcntsw();\n    for(int i=0;i<n;i+=2*vl) {\n        svbool_t pi0=svwhilelt_b32(i,n),pi1=svwhilelt_b32(i+vl,n);\n        for(int j=0;j<n;j+=2*vl) {\n            svbool_t pj0=svwhilelt_b32(j,n),pj1=svwhilelt_b32(j+vl,n);\n            svzero_za();\n            for(int k=0;k<n;k++) {\n                svfloat32_t a0=svld1_f32(pi0,at+(size_t)k*n+i);\n                svfloat32_t a1=svld1_f32(pi1,at+(size_t)k*n+(i+vl<n?i+vl:i));\n                svfloat32_t b0=svld1_f32(pj0,b+(size_t)k*n+j);\n                svfloat32_t b1=svld1_f32(pj1,b+(size_t)k*n+(j+vl<n?j+vl:j));\n                svmopa_za32_f32_m(0,pi0,pj0,a0,b0);\n                svmopa_za32_f32_m(1,pi0,pj1,a0,b1);\n                svmopa_za32_f32_m(2,pi1,pj0,a1,b0);\n                svmopa_za32_f32_m(3,pi1,pj1,a1,b1);\n            }\n            for(int r=0;r<vl && i+r<n;r++) {\n                svst1_hor_za32(0,r,pj0,c+(size_t)(i+r)*n+j);\n                if(j+vl<n) svst1_hor_za32(1,r,pj1,c+(size_t)(i+r)*n+j+vl);\n            }\n            for(int r=0;r<vl && i+vl+r<n;r++) {\n                svst1_hor_za32(2,r,pj0,c+(size_t)(i+vl+r)*n+j);\n                if(j+vl<n) svst1_hor_za32(3,r,pj1,c+(size_t)(i+vl+r)*n+j+vl);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\nstatic void fallback_gemm(int n,const float *restrict A,const float *restrict B,float *restrict C) {\n    if(n<=0)return;\n    float *at=malloc((size_t)n*n*sizeof(float));\n    if(!at) {\n
